Detailed function implementation:

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def lse_core(a, beta, N=None):
    n = len(a)
    if N is None: N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c_full = np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)
    c = c_full[:2*n-1]
    # c may have tiny negative due to roundoff; clip to 0? Since a nonnegative, c nonnegative. Clip.
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <=0: return 0, zeros
    s = beta*(c-cmax)
    s = np.where(s < -50, np.exp(-50), np.exp(s))? Use np.exp(np.clip(s,-50,50))
    Z = exps.sum()
    L = cmax + np.log(Z)/beta
    p = exps/Z
    a_rev = a[::-1]
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    AR = np.fft.rfft(a_rev, N)
    h_full = np.fft.irfft(AR*P, N)
    h = h_full[n-1:2*n-1]
    # h should be nonnegative? Not necessarily? p,a nonneg so h nonneg.
    # gradient of L: 2h
    return L, cmax, p, h

def normalized_lse_objective(a, beta):

    n = len(a)

    b = a / S
    # maybe clip negative tiny: b = np.maximum(b,0); S2=b.sum(); if S2 !=1 b/=S2 (but if a nonneg by bounds, ok)

Let's propose final algorithm summary in docstring:
- Use exact FFT evaluation of self-convolution.
- Optimize scale-invariant LSE approximation on probability simplex using correct analytic gradients and L-BFGS-B.
- Multi-phase: medium-size global starts, parent polish with beta annealing, symmetrization/power transforms, final high-beta polishing and coordinate-like multiplicative polishing.
- Return best verified sequence.

def estimate_objective_cost(a, beta=1000.0):
    if a is None or len(a)<16: return 0.02
    a = normalize_probs(a)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    reps=5
    for _ in range(reps):
        f,g = lse_objective(a, beta)
    return max((time.monotonic()-t0)/reps, 1e-4)
```

def run_lbfgs_beta(a, beta, time_budget, eval_cost, maxfun_cap=80000, maxfun_min=500):
    a = normalize_probs(a)
    if a is None: return compute_exact_score(a), a
    maxfun = int(time_budget / max(eval_cost,1e-5) / 1.5)
    maxfun = int(np.clip(maxfun, maxfun_min, maxfun_cap))
    maxiter = max(50, min(20000, maxfun//3 + 20))
    options = {'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-10,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':50,'m':50}
    bounds = [(0,1)]*n
    try:
       res = minimize(lse_objective, a.copy(), args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=True, options=options)
       a_opt = np.maximum(res.x,0)
    except Exception:
       return compute_exact_score(a), a
    a_opt = normalize_probs(a_opt)
    if a_opt is None: return compute_exact_score(a), a
    sc = compute_exact_score(a_opt)
    # symmetrize maybe after return? Do inside?
    return sc, a_opt
```

def generate_medium_starts(n, rng):
   starts=[]
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   for slope in (-3,-1.5,0,1.5,3):
       a=np.exp(slope*(x-0.5))
       a += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)? maybe no noise
       starts.append(normalize(a))
   for _ in range(4):
       a=np.ones(n)
       for m in range(1,6):
          amp = rng.uniform(0.02,0.15)
          ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
          a += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x+ph)
       a=np.abs(a)? or a=np.maximum(a,0)+1e-6
       starts.append(normalize(a))
   for _ in range(2):
       a=np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))+0.1
       # smooth
       if n>16: kernel=np.exp(-((np.arange(-10,11))/5)**2/2); a=np.convolve(a,kernel,'same')
       starts.append(normalize(a))
   return [s for s in starts if s is not None]
```

Let's code `_normalize_probs`:
```
def _normalize_probs(a):
    if a is None: return None
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return None
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s<1e-12: return None
    arr=arr/s
    return arr
```
But if arr values >1? For probability, max≤1 after normalize if sum positive? If one element 1000 sum 1000, normalized 1. Good.

`_exact_score` uses `_fast_conv` which clips c to nonnegative. If b sum1. Good.

`_fast_conv` with N maybe None. For n=1, M=1, N=1. rfft length1 okay. h slicing? n-1=0 to 1. fine.

`_lse_info`:
```
def _lse_info(a, beta, N=None):
    n=len(a); c,N2=...; cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 1e-300: return 0.0,0.0,np.zeros(n)
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax); clipped=np.clip(scaled,-50,50); exps=np.exp(clipped)
    Z=float(exps.sum()); p=exps/Z
    L=cmax + np.log(Z)/beta
    ar=a[::-1]
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N2); AR=np.fft.rfft(ar,N2)
    hfull=np.fft.irfft(P*AR,N2)
    h=hfull[n-1:2*n-1]
    # Due to numerical, h may have tiny neg; clip
    if h.shape[0]!=n: h=... (should)
    h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
    return L,cmax,h
```
For high beta, exps of top near 1, subpeaks underflow to 0; p normalized. Good.

`_lse_objective`:
```
def _lse_objective(a, beta):
    n=len(a)
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S<1e-12:
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    b=a/S
    # clip tiny neg
    if np.any(b < 0): b=np.maximum(b,0)
    S2=float(b.sum())
    if S2<1e-12: return 1e100, zeros
    if S2 != 1.0: b=b/S2
    # Maybe ensure upper? no
    L,cmax,h=_lse_info(b,beta)
    if not np.isfinite(L): return 1e100, zeros
    K=2.0*n
    f=K*L
    grad=...
    if not finite grad: ...
    return f, grad
```
Should we include cmax exact in f? LSE for beta finite > exact max. Good.

`_run_lbfgs_beta`: Need avoid passing bounds list huge repeatedly? Create inside.
```
def _run_lbfgs_beta(a, beta, time_budget, eval_cost, maxfun_cap=80000, gtol=1e-9):
    a0=_normalize_probs(a)
    if a0 is None: return 0, None? return inf
    n=len(a0)
    maxfun=int(time_budget / max(eval_cost,1e-5) / 2.0)
    maxfun=max(500, min(maxfun_cap, maxfun))
    maxiter=max(50, min(30000, maxfun//4 + 100))
    options = {...}
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
       res=minimize(...)
    except Exception as e:
       return compute_exact_score(a0), a0
    x=res.x
    x=np.maximum(x,0)
    a_opt=_normalize_probs(x)
    if a_opt is None: return compute_exact_score(a0), a0
    sc=compute_exact_score(a_opt)
    # symmetrize
    a_sym=0.5*(a_opt+a_opt[::-1])
    a_sym=_normalize_probs(a_sym)
    if a_sym is not None:
       sc_sym=compute_exact_score(a_sym)
       if sc_sym<sc:
          sc=sc_sym; a_opt=a_sym
    return sc,a_opt
```

def _multi_beta_polish(a, betas, deadline, eval_cost, maxfun_cap=80000, gtol=1e-9):
    a=_normalize_probs(a)
    if a is None: return np.inf,None
    best_sc=compute_exact_score(a); best_a=a.copy()
    n=len(betas)
    for i,beta in enumerate(betas):
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem<20: break
       weights=[2**j for j in range(i,n)]
       total_w=sum(weights)
       alloc=max(15.0, 0.95*rem * weights[0]/total_w)
       sc,a_opt=_run_lbfgs_beta(a,beta,alloc,eval_cost,maxfun_cap,gtol)
       if a_opt is not None:
          # try sym
          a_sym=...
          if score_sym<sc: ...
          if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a_opt
          # choose continuation
          a_score = compute_exact_score(a_opt) # sc already
          if a_score < best_sc + 0.05:
              a=a_opt
          else:
              a=best_a
       print
    return best_sc,best_a
```

def _multiplicative_polish(a, beta, time_budget_max, eval_cost, max_iters=2000):

If j from j_start to j_end, k-j ranges k-j_start down to k-j_end. We can take segment `a[k-j_start:k-j_end+1][::-1]`. Add to h. Then divide top_k. Good.
- Update a *= exp(-eta * h_scaled). This is subgradient direction with equal weights on top peaks. Could be very effective for final. Since gradient of max with subgradient uniform top active. Use backtracking/adapt.
This avoids FFT for h; only fast_conv each iter. Exact score also needs c; we can compute c once per iter and use for exact max. Good.

Implement:
```
def _peak_multiplicative_polish(a, beta? no, top_k=5, n_iters=1000, eta_init=0.1, deadline=None):
    a=normalize; best=score(a); best_a=a; eta=eta_init
    n=len(a)
    for it in range(n_iters):
       if deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       c,N=fast_conv(a)
       M=float(c.max()); exact=2*n*M
       if exact<best: best=exact; best_a=a.copy()
       # top k sorted
       if len(c)<=top_k: idx=np.arange(len(c)) else idx=np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
       h=np.zeros(n)
       for k in idx:
          k=int(k)
          js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
          if je>=js:
             # h[js:je+1] += a[k-js:k-je+1][::-1]
             h[js:je+1] += a[k-js:k-je+1][::-1]
       h /= len(idx)
       hmax=float(h.max())
       if hmax <=1e-30: break
       h = h/hmax
       a_new = a * np.exp(-eta*h)
       a_new = normalize(a_new)
       if a_new is None: break
       sc=exact_score(a_new)
       if sc < best - 1e-12:
           best_a=a_new; best=sc; a=a_new; eta = min(eta*1.25,1.5)
       else:
           eta *= 0.7
           if eta < 1e-5: break
    return best,best_a
```

Now, let's refine L-BFGS objective gradient with h. Need ensure h is gradient of L (not scaled). In `_lse_info`, we return h = ∑_m b_m p_{m+j}. Then ∂L/∂b_j = 2h_j. In objective, `grad_b = 4.0*n*h`? For f=K L, K=2n, grad=K*2h=4n h. Yes.
Then derivative wrt raw a: grad = grad_b/S - b*dot/S^2 + penalty. Let's verify dot = b·grad_b. Since grad_b is gradient wrt b of f(G(b)). Then ∂f/∂a_j = ∑_i grad_b_i ∂b_i/∂a_j. With b_i=a_i/S. ∂b_i/∂a_j=δij/S - a_i/S^2. So grad_j=grad_b_j/S - (a_j/S^2)∑_i grad_b_i a_i/S? Wait ∑_i grad_b_i a_i/S^2 = (grad_b·a)/S^2. But a_i = S b_i, so = S(grad_b·b)/S^2=(grad_b·b)/S. Thus second term = b_j * (grad_b·b)/S. Compare earlier I had b*dot/S^2. Let's recalc carefully:
∂f/∂a_j = grad_b_j / S - (1/S^2) a_j (grad_b·a)
= grad_b_j/S - (S b_j / S^2)(grad_b·a)
grad_b·a = S (grad_b·b)
=> = grad_b_j/S - b_j (grad_b·b)/S.
So grad = (grad_b - b*dot)/S, where dot = b·grad_b. Not b*dot/S^2. Let's check scale invariance: grad·a = (1/S)(grad_b·a - dot*S?) = (1/S)(S dot - S dot)=0. Good. My earlier formula wrong by S factor! Let's derive with direct: if S=1, both same. If S not 1, critical. Use correct:
```
dot = np.dot(b, grad_b)
grad = (grad_b - b*dot) / S + penalty
```
This magnitude if S=1 O(1). If S drifts, grad scales 1/S; penalty helps.

Now, if we add penalty gamma(S-1)^2, grad += 2*gamma*(S-1)*ones.

Let's update `_lse_objective` accordingly.

Also, objective f=K*L. But if a sum S not 1, b normalized, f independent of S except penalty. Gradient formula uses S. Good.

Now, if S very small, grad huge; but bounds and initial avoid. Add if S<1e-6 return big, zeros.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    import time? Already imports top.
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=float(budget_s)
    usable=max(60.0, total_budget-120.0)
    final_deadline=t_start+total_budget-30.0 # leave 30 for final verify
    main_deadline=t_start+0.62*usable
    medium_deadline=t_start+0.80*usable
    variant_deadline=t_start+0.92*usable
    final_deadline=t_start+usable

    # load parent
    parent=None
    if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
       p=_normalize_probs(globals()['height_sequence_1'])
       if p is not None: parent=p
    if parent is None:
       # create start medium and optimize a bit? Or default length 4096 uniform? We'll use random smooth at 4096.
       parent=np.ones(4096)/4096

    best_a=parent.copy(); best_sc=_exact_score(best_a)
    top_candidates=[best_a.copy()]

    # quick sym
    sym=normalize(0.5*(parent+parent[::-1])); if score<best update; add.

    # estimate cost
    eval_cost=_estimate_objective_cost(best_a,1000.0)
    print
    # main phase
    if best_sc > 1.5027 or True:
       betas_main=[100,1000,5000,20000,100000,500000,2000000]
       best_sc,best_a=_multi_beta_polish(best_a, betas_main, main_deadline, eval_cost, maxfun_cap=60000, gtol=1e-9)
       top_candidates.append(best_a)

    # if no parent or score poor, medium
    if time.monotonic() < medium_deadline and best_sc > 1.502 - maybe:
       med_sc, med_a = _medium_search(medium_deadline, rng, target_len=len(parent))
       if med_a is not None:
          # interpolate to full length
          med_full=_interpolate(med_a, len(best_a))
          sc_full=_exact_score(med_full); if sc_full<best... maybe run quick polish
          if _time < variant_deadline:
             best_med,med_full=_multi_beta_polish(med_full,[5000,50000,500000], min(variant_deadline, time+600), eval_cost, maxfun_cap=30000)
             update
    # variants from parent/best
    if best_sc > 1.502? Always try if time
       for cand in generate_variants(best_a,parent,rng):
          if time>variant_deadline: break
          sc=_exact_score(cand); update; add top
          if sc < best_sc + 0.05:
             # quick L-BFGS one beta
             sc2,cand2=_run_lbfgs_beta(cand,50000, min(variant_deadline, time+120), eval_cost, maxfun_cap=20000)
             update
    # final high polish
    if time< final_deadline-30:
       betas_final=[200000,1000000,5000000]
       best_sc,best_a=_multi_beta_polish(best_a, betas_final, final_deadline-20, eval_cost, maxfun_cap=80000, gtol=1e-10)
    # peak multiplicative final
    if time< final_deadline-10:
       for topk in [5,10,20]:
          if time>final_deadline-10: break
          t_end=min(final_deadline-5, time+240)

          update

       out=[float(x) for x in normalize(cand)]
       if sum(out)<0.01: continue
       try: v=evaluate_sequence(out)
       except Exception: v=np.inf
       print
       if v<best_verified: best_out=out; best_verified=v
    if best_out is None: best_out=[float(x) for x in best_a]
    print final; return best_out

def _generate_variants(a, b, rng, max_count=8):
   res=[]
   for c in [a,b]:
     if c is None continue
     s=normalize(0.5*(c+c[::-1])); res.append(s)
     for alpha in [0.7,1.3]:
       arr=np.power(np.maximum(c,1e-15),alpha); res.append(normalize(arr))
     # smooth
     if len(c)>32:
       kernel=np.exp(-((np.arange(-5,6)/3.0)**2)/2); kernel/=kernel.sum(); arr=np.convolve(c,kernel,'same'); res.append(normalize(arr))
     # random perturb
     arr=c*(1.0+0.05*rng.standard_normal(len(c))); res.append(normalize(arr))
   # unique by score maybe not; just max 12
   return [x for x in res if x is not None][:12]
```

Now, `_peak_multiplicative_polish` details:

Code:
```
def _peak_multiplicative_polish(a, top_k, deadline=None, n_iters=5000, eta=0.2):
   a=normalize(a); if None return inf,None
   best=exact_score(a); best_a=a; eta_current=eta
   n=len(a)
   for it in range(n_iters):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       c,N=fast_conv(a)
       M=float(c.max()); sc=2*n*M
       if sc<best: best=sc; best_a=a.copy(); continue? we can still update.
       if len(c) <= top_k: idx=np.arange(len(c))
       else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
       h=np.zeros(n)
       for k in idx:
          k=int(k); js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
          if je>=js:
             h[js:je+1] += a[k-js:k-je+1][::-1]
       h /= float(len(idx))
       hmax=float(h.max())
       if hmax <= 1e-30: break
       h = h/hmax
       # To avoid making all too small, exp(-eta*h) min exp(-eta)
       a_new = a * np.exp(-eta_current*h)
       a_new = normalize(a_new)
       if a_new is None: break
       sc_new=exact_score(a_new)
       if sc_new < best - 1e-12:
           best=sc_new; best_a=a_new.copy(); a=a_new
           eta_current=min(eta_current*1.2, 1.5)
       else:
           eta_current*=0.7
           if eta_current < 1e-5: break
   return best,best_a
```
This only decreases entries with high contribution. If `eta_current` large, may distort. Backtracking adapt. Could also try positive? Gradient descent only. Maybe sometimes increasing high contribution? No, to reduce peaks, decreasing components is right because all products nonnegative; increasing only raises peaks. So multiplicative decrease with relative differences.

But if top peaks all share common a_j, decreasing it may reduce sum but also reduce all; normalization rescales adding mass to less-contributing, good.

We can run with top_k=3,5,10. For top_k too large, direction may raise many inactive. Start with 3/5.

Could also take step with `h - h.mean()`? Since normalization removes uniform scale, using h or h-hmin same. h normalized max 1; entries with h=0 increase relative by factor 1/exp(-0)= wait exp(-eta*h): h=0 factor1, h=max factor e^{-eta}; after normalization, zeros increase relative. Good. If all h same, no change. Fine.

Detailed code sketches:

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    m=max(1,int(m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
For m=1, (0).bit_length=0 ->1.

```
def _fast_conv(a, N=None):
    n=len(a)
    if N is None: N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    if c.size:
       c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c,N
```
`np.fft.irfft` returns length N; if small imaginary round. Good.

`_lse_info`: when computing h, if n==0? no.

```
def _lse_info(a,beta,N=None):
    n=len(a)
    c,N2=_fast_conv(a,N)
    cmax=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
       return 0.0,0.0,np.zeros(n)
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    exps=np.exp(np.clip(scaled,-50.0,50.0))
    Z=float(exps.sum())
    if Z<=0: exps.fill(1.0); Z=len(c)
    p=exps/Z
    L=cmax + float(np.log(Z))/beta
    ar=a[::-1]
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N2)
    AR=np.fft.rfft(ar,N2)
    hfull=np.fft.irfft(P*AR,N2)
    h=hfull[n-1:2*n-1].copy()
    if h.size != n:
       h_full2 = np.resize(h_full, n)? not needed.
    h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
    return L,cmax,h
```
Potential: For n large, hfull slice length n. Good.

`_compute_exact_score`:
```
def _compute_exact_score(a):
    a=_normalize_probs(a)
    if a is None: return np.inf
    n=len(a)
    c,N=_fast_conv(a)
    M=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    return float(2.0*n*M)
```
Since normalized. Good.

`_estimate_objective_cost`:
```
def _estimate_objective_cost(a,beta=1000.0):
    a=_normalize_probs(a)
    if a is None: return 0.02
    reps=5
    t0=time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(reps):
       try: _lse_objective(a,beta)
       except Exception: pass
    dt=(time.monotonic()-t0)/reps
    if not finite or dt<=0: dt=0.02
    return max(dt,1e-5)
```

`_normalize_probs`: If input list of Python floats, okay. Clip to [0,1000] but if sequence scale arbitrary. For L-BFGS raw x, values should be probability-like; clipping upper 1000 not needed. Use `np.clip(arr,0.0,1e100)`? If arr contains 1000 and sum 1e6, normalized 1e-3. Fine. If arr has inf, reject. Since raw x bounds [0,2], no huge.

```
def _normalize_probs(a):
    if a is None: return None
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return None
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1e6)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return None
    return arr/s
```

But if parent values raw large (clamped 1000), normalize okay. For softmax? no.

`_run_lbfgs_beta`:
```
def _run_lbfgs_beta(a,beta,time_budget,eval_cost,maxfun_cap=80000,gtol=1e-9):
    a0=_normalize_probs(a)
    if a0 is None: return np.inf,None
    sc0=_compute_exact_score(a0)
    if minimize is None:
       # fallback multiplicative a few
       sc,ao=_peak_multiplicative_polish(a0,5,deadline=time.monotonic()+min(time_budget,10),n_iters=200)
       return sc,ao
    n=len(a0)
    maxfun=int(time_budget / max(eval_cost,1e-5) / 2.5)
    maxfun=max(300, min(maxfun_cap, maxfun))
    maxiter=max(50, min(50000, maxfun//3 + 100))
    bounds=np.array([(0.0,2.0)]*n?) minimize accepts list; list fine.
    options={'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':gtol,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':50,'m':30}
    try:
       res=minimize(_lse_objective, a0.copy(), args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=True, options=options)
       x=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       return sc0,a0.copy()
    aopt=_normalize_probs(np.maximum(x,0.0))
    if aopt is None: return sc0,a0.copy()
    sc=_compute_exact_score(aopt)
    # sym
    sym=_normalize_probs(0.5*(aopt+aopt[::-1]))
    if sym is not None:
       scs=_compute_exact_score(sym)
       if scs<sc:
          sc=scs; aopt=sym
    return sc,aopt
```
If time_budget small, maxfun min 300 may still exceed time; okay.

`_multi_beta_polish`:
```
def _multi_beta_polish(a,betas,deadline,eval_cost,maxfun_cap=80000,gtol=1e-9):
   a=_normalize_probs(a)
   if a is None: return np.inf,None
   best_sc=_compute_exact_score(a); best_a=a.copy()
   n=len(betas)
   for i,beta in enumerate(betas):
      rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
      if rem<20: break
      weights=[2.0**j for j in range(i,n)]
      total_w=sum(weights)
      alloc=max(10.0, 0.95*rem*weights[0]/total_w)
      # allocate max alloc maybe 900? no
      sc,aopt=_run_lbfgs_beta(a,beta,alloc,eval_cost,maxfun_cap,gtol)
      if aopt is not None:
         # maybe sym again inside
         if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc=sc; best_a=aopt.copy()
         # continuation choice
         cont_sc=sc
         if cont_sc < best_sc + 0.05:
             a=aopt
         else:
             a=best_a
      print(...) if desired (top-level can print)
   return best_sc,best_a
```
Printing in function okay. But too many prints? okay.

Need avoid `2.0**j` for j maybe 20 (large but n<=10). fine.

`_generate_medium_starts`:
```
def _generate_medium_starts(n, rng):
    starts=[]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    for slope in (-2.0,-1.0,0.0,1.0,2.0,4.0):
       a=np.exp(slope*(x-0.5))
       a=a + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
       starts.append(_normalize_probs(a))
    for m in range(4):
       a=np.ones(n)
       for k in range(1,7):
          amp=rng.uniform(0.02,0.2)
          ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
          a += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x+ph)
       starts.append(_normalize_probs(a))
    for _ in range(3):
       a=np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))+0.1
       if n>32:
          w=np.arange(-10,11)
          std=3.0
          k=np.exp(-0.5*(w/std)**2); k/=k.sum()
          a=np.convolve(a,k,mode='same')
       starts.append(_normalize_probs(a))
    # add anti-symmetric ramp? slopes done.
    out=[s for s in starts if s is not None]
    # unique? no
    return out[:12]
```
Maybe noise in exponential makes nonnegative after clip. Good.

Pseudo:
```
return_deadline = t_start + total_budget - 5
best_verified = None; best_ver_score=inf
for attempt in range(3):
   if time > return_deadline - 20: break
   pool=[best_a]
   if parent is not None: pool.append(parent)
   sym=_normalize(0.5*(best_a+best_a[::-1])); if sym pool.append(sym)
   # maybe a pool from top_candidates (unique)

      out=[float(x) for x in _normalize_probs(cand)]
      if len(out)==0 or sum(out)<0.01: continue
      v=evaluate_sequence(out)
      if np.isfinite(v) and v<best_ver_score:
         best_ver_score=v; best_verified=out
   if best_ver_score <= 1.5027 - 1e-7: break
   # polish using best_verified or best_a
   base=_normalize_probs(best_verified if best_verified is not None else best_a)
   if base is None: base=best_a
   t_end=min(return_deadline-20, time.monotonic()+240)
   if t_end > time.monotonic()+30:
      sc,bnew=_multi_beta_polish(base,[200000,1000000], t_end, eval_cost, maxfun_cap=50000, gtol=1e-10)
      if bnew is not None: best_a=... if sc<best
      # peak quick
      if time< t_end: ...
```

Let's code:
```
def _run_lbfgs_beta(...):
   a0=normalize; best_sc=exact; best_a=a0
   remaining=time_budget
   t_total=time
   for chunk in range(10):
      if remaining < 15: break
      maxfun=int(remaining/max(eval_cost,1e-5)/2.0)
      maxfun=max(300,min(chunk_cap,maxfun))
      maxiter=max(50,min(50000,maxfun//3+100))
      t0=time.monotonic()
      try: res=scipy_minimize(...)
      except Exception: break
      dt=time.monotonic()-t0
      remaining -= dt
      # update a0 from res.x
      aopt=normalize(max(res.x,0))
      if aopt is not None:
         sc=exact; sym...
         if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=aopt
         a0=aopt
      # convergence check
      msg=getattr(res,'message','')
      if 'succeeded' in msg or 'success' in msg or 'gtol' in msg or 'ftol' in msg or 'infsoc' in msg: break
      if maxfun < 300: break
   return best_sc,best_a
```

Function `_normalize_probs`:
```
def _normalize_probs(a):
    if a is None:
        return None
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1.0e6)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1.0e-12:
        return None
    return arr / s
```
If parent raw values all >1e6? clip to 1e6, still positive. But evaluate clamps to1000; if we normalize parent after clipping to1e6, scale changes? If raw parent had values >1e6? likely not. However the convolution coefficient c_k includes both ordered pairs, but derivative of each term both equal b_{k-j}; total 2b_{k-j}. Our ∂L/∂b_j should be ∑ p_k 2b_{k-j} for each k where valid and k≠2j? For k=2j, derivative is 2b_j =2b_{k-j}. So in all cases ∂c_k/∂b_j = 2 b_{k-j} if 0≤k-j<n. There is no additional b_j. ```
def _interpolate_sequence(a, n_target):
    a=_normalize_probs(a)
    if a is None: return None
    if len(a)==n_target: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,len(a))
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_target)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return _normalize_probs(b)
```

```python
"""
Search for low-peak self-convolution sequences.

The objective is scale-invariant and can be written, after normalizing the
sequence to sum one, as 2n * max(a * a).  This implementation optimizes a
smooth log-sum-exp approximation of the maximum convolution coefficient using
correct analytic gradients on the probability simplex.  It combines:
  * FFT-evaluated self-convolution,
  * L-BFGS-B polishing with a beta schedule,
  * medium-size global starts,
  * symmetrization / power-transform variants,
  * final active-peak multiplicative polishing,
  * final verification with the provided evaluate_sequence.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    scipy_minimize = None


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = max(1, int(m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize_probs(a):
    """Cut to the evaluation clamp [0, 1000], normalize to sum 1."""
    if a is None:
        return None
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1.0e-12:
        return None
    return arr / s


def _fast_conv(a, N=None):
    """Linear self-convolution using FFT."""
    n = len(a)
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, N


def _lse_info(a, beta, N=None):
    """
    Return (L, cmax, h) where L is the log-sum-exp smooth maximum of a*a,
    and h_j = sum_m a_m p_{m+j}.  The gradient of L wrt a is 2*h.
    """
    n = len(a)
    c, N2 = _fast_conv(a, N)
    cmax = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if cmax <= 1.0e-300:
        return 0.0, 0.0, np.zeros(n)

    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -50.0, 50.0)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    Z = float(exps.sum())
    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        exps = np.ones_like(c)
        Z = float(exps.size)
    p = exps / Z

    L = cmax + float(np.log(Z)) / beta

    ar = a[::-1]
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N2)
    AR = np.fft.rfft(ar, N2)
    hfull = np.fft.irfft(P * AR, N2)
    h = hfull[n - 1:2 * n - 1].copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.zeros(n)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    return L, cmax, h


def _compute_exact_score(a):
    a = _normalize_probs(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.inf
    n = len(a)
    c, _ = _fast_conv(a)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def _estimate_objective_cost(a, beta=1000.0):
    a = _normalize_probs(a)
    if a is None:
        return 0.02
    reps = 5
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(reps):
        try:
            _lse_objective(a, beta)
        except Exception:
            pass
    dt = (time.monotonic() - t0) / float(reps)
    if not np.isfinite(dt) or dt <= 0.0:
        dt = 0.02
    return max(dt, 1.0e-5)


def _lse_objective(a, beta):
    """
    Scale-invariant smooth objective:
        f(x) = 2n * L(a / sum(a)) + 1e-8 * (sum(x)-1)^2
    The tiny sum penalty keeps the representation scale near one.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1.0e-12:
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n)

    b = a / S
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    S2 = float(b.sum())
    if S2 < 1.0e-12:
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n)
    b = b / S2

    L, cmax, h = _lse_info(b, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(L):
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n)

    K = 2.0 * float(n)
    f = K * L

    # Gradient wrt b of K*L is K * 2h.
    grad_b = 4.0 * float(n) * h
    dot = float(np.dot(b, grad_b))

    # Chain rule for b = a/S.
    grad = (grad_b - b * dot) / S

    # Mild sum penalty.
    gamma = 1.0e-8
    grad = grad + (2.0 * gamma * (S - 1.0))

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n)
    return f, grad


def _run_lbfgs_beta(a, beta, time_budget, eval_cost, maxfun_cap=80000, gtol=1.0e-9):
    """
    Run L-BFGS for one beta, possibly in chunks to use the allocated time.
    Returns (best_score, best_sequence, last_sequence).
    """
    a0 = _normalize_probs(a)
    if a0 is None:
        return np.inf, None, None

    best_sc = _compute_exact_score(a0)
    best_a = a0.copy()

    if scipy_minimize is None:
        # Fallback if scipy is unavailable: simple peak multiplicative polish.
        end = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, min(float(time_budget), 60.0))
        sc, ao = _peak_multiplicative_polish(a0, 5, deadline=end, n_iters=2000, eta=0.2)
        if ao is not None and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc, best_a = sc, ao
        return best_sc, best_a, ao

    remaining = max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    n = len(a0)

    for _chunk in range(12):
        if remaining < 15.0:
            break

        maxfun = int(remaining / max(eval_cost, 1.0e-5) / 2.5)
        maxfun = max(300, min(int(maxfun_cap), maxfun))
        maxiter = max(50, min(50000, maxfun // 3 + 100))

        bounds = [(0.0, 2.0)] * n
        options = {
            "ftol": 1.0e-15,
            "gtol": gtol,
            "maxiter": maxiter,
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "maxls": 50,
            "m": 30,
        }

        t0 = time.monotonic()
        try:
            res = scipy_minimize(
                _lse_objective,
                a0.copy(),
                args=(beta,),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                jac=True,
                options=options,
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        dt = time.monotonic() - t0
        remaining -= dt

        try:
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            break

        aopt = _normalize_probs(np.maximum(x, 0.0))
        if aopt is None:
            break

        sc = _compute_exact_score(aopt)

        sym = _normalize_probs(0.5 * (aopt + aopt[::-1]))
        if sym is not None:
            sc_sym = _compute_exact_score(sym)
            if sc_sym < sc:
                sc = sc_sym
                aopt = sym

        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = aopt.copy()

        # Continue from the new point for the next chunk.
        a0 = aopt

        status = int(getattr(res, "status", -1))
        msg = str(getattr(res, "message", ""))
        if status == 0:
            break
        if "successfully" in msg or "terminated successfully" in msg:
            break
        if "gtol" in msg or "ftol" in msg or "infsoc" in msg:
            break
        if maxfun < 300:
            break

    return best_sc, best_a, a0


def _multi_beta_polish(a, betas, deadline, eval_cost, maxfun_cap=80000, gtol=1.0e-9):
    """
    Anneal beta, spending more time on higher beta values.
    Returns (best_score, best_sequence).
    """
    a = _normalize_probs(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.inf, None

    best_sc = _compute_exact_score(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    n_betas = len(betas)

    for i, beta in enumerate(betas):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 20.0:
            break

        weights = [2.0 ** j for j in range(i, n_betas)]
        total_w = float(sum(weights))
        alloc = 0.95 * rem * (float(weights[0]) / total_w)
        alloc = max(10.0, alloc)

        sc, aopt, lasta = _run_lbfgs_beta(
            a, beta, alloc, eval_cost,
            maxfun_cap=maxfun_cap, gtol=gtol
        )

        if aopt is not None and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = aopt.copy()

        # Choose continuation point among known local states.
        states = []
        if aopt is not None:
            states.append((_compute_exact_score(aopt), aopt))
        if lasta is not None:
            slast = _compute_exact_score(lasta)
            states.append((slast, lasta))

        allowed = best_sc + 0.05
        chosen = None
        chosen_sc = np.inf
        for s, st in states:
            if s < allowed and s < chosen_sc:
                chosen = st
                chosen_sc = s

        if chosen is not None:
            a = chosen.copy()
        else:
            a = best_a.copy()

        print(f"  LSE beta={beta}: local best={best_sc:.10f}")

    return best_sc, best_a


def _peak_multiplicative_polish(a, top_k, deadline=None, n_iters=5000, eta=0.2):
    """
    Simple exact-subgradient multiplicative polish on the top convolution peaks.
    """
    a = _normalize_probs(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.inf, None

    best_sc = _compute_exact_score(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    n = len(a)
    eta_cur = float(eta)
    top_k = max(1, int(top_k))

    for _ in range(n_iters):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c, _ = _fast_conv(a)
        M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        sc = 2.0 * n * M
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        if top_k >= c.size:
            idx = np.arange(c.size)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, c.size - top_k)[c.size - top_k:]

        h = np.zeros(n)
        for k in idx:
            k = int(k)
            js = max(0, k - n + 1)
            je = min(k, n - 1)
            if je >= js:
                h[js:je + 1] += a[k - je:k - js + 1][::-1]
        h /= float(len(idx))

        hmax = float(h.max()) if h.size else 0.0
        if hmax <= 1.0e-30:
            break

        h = h / hmax
        a_new = a * np.exp(-eta_cur * h)
        a_new = _normalize_probs(a_new)
        if a_new is None:
            break

        sc_new = _compute_exact_score(a_new)
        if sc_new < best_sc - 1.0e-12:
            best_sc = sc_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            a = a_new
            eta_cur = min(eta_cur * 1.25, 1.5)
        else:
            eta_cur *= 0.7
            if eta_cur < 1.0e-5:
                break

    return best_sc, best_a


def _interpolate_sequence(a, n_target):
    a = _normalize_probs(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if len(a) == n_target:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(a))
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_target)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return _normalize_probs(b)


def _generate_medium_starts(n, rng):
    starts = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    for slope in (-2.5, -1.25, 0.0, 1.25, 2.5, 4.0):
        a = np.exp(slope * (x - 0.5))
        a = a + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n)
        starts.append(_normalize_probs(a))

    for _ in range(4):
        a = np.ones(n)
        for k in range(1, 7):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.02, 0.20)
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph)
        starts.append(_normalize_probs(a))

    for _ in range(3):
        a = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.1
        if n > 32:
            w = np.arange(-12, 13)
            std = 3.0
            ker = np.exp(-0.5 * (w / std) ** 2)
            ker = ker / ker.sum()
            a = np.convolve(a, ker, mode="same")
        starts.append(_normalize_probs(a))

    out = []
    for s in starts:
        if s is not None:
            out.append(s)
    return out[:14]


def _medium_search(max_deadline, rng):
    """
    Medium-size global exploration.  Kept intentionally time-bounded.
    """
    if time.monotonic() > max_deadline - 30.0:
        return np.inf, None

    best_sc = np.inf
    best_a = None

    if max_deadline - time.monotonic() > 350.0:
        sizes = [512, 1024, 2048]
    else:
        sizes = [512, 1024]

    for nmed in sizes:
        if time.monotonic() > max_deadline - 30.0:
            break

        starts = _generate_medium_starts(nmed, rng)
        for s in starts:
            if time.monotonic() > max_deadline - 20.0:
                break
            sc0 = _compute_exact_score(s)
            if best_sc < np.inf and sc0 > best_sc + 0.20:
                continue

            sub_deadline = min(max_deadline, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
            if sub_deadline - time.monotonic() < 5.0:
                continue

            cost = _estimate_objective_cost(s, 2000.0)
            sc, ao = _multi_beta_polish(
                s,
                [200, 2000, 20000, 100000],
                sub_deadline,
                cost,
                maxfun_cap=3000,
                gtol=1.0e-8,
            )
            if ao is not None and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = ao.copy()
                print(f"  medium n={nmed}: {sc:.10f}")

    return best_sc, best_a


def _generate_variants(a, b, rng, max_count=14):
    res = []
    for c in (a, b):
        c = _normalize_probs(c)
        if c is None:
            continue

        res.append(c.copy())

        sym = _normalize_probs(0.5 * (c + c[::-1]))
        if sym is not None:
            res.append(sym)

        for alpha in (0.70, 1.30):
            arr = np.power(np.maximum(c, 1.0e-15), alpha)
            res.append(_normalize_probs(arr))

        if c.size > 32:
            w = np.arange(-6, 7)
            ker = np.exp(-0.5 * (w / 3.0) ** 2)
            ker = ker / ker.sum()
            sm = np.convolve(c, ker, mode="same")
            res.append(_normalize_probs(sm))

        noisy = c * (1.0 + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(c.size))
        res.append(_normalize_probs(noisy))

    out = []
    seen = []
    for x in res:
        if x is not None:
            # Very rough deduplication by length and sum-of-squares.
            key = (len(x), round(float(np.dot(x, x)), 12))
            if key not in seen:
                seen.append(key)
                out.append(x)
    if len(out) > max_count:
        out = out[:max_count]
    return out


def _safe_evaluate(out):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(out)
    except Exception:
        val = np.inf
    try:
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        val = np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Main search entry point."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(300.0, float(budget_s))
    usable = max(90.0, total_budget - 120.0)

    # Absolute deadline before final output.
    return_deadline = t_start + total_budget - 25.0
    main_deadline = t_start + 0.62 * usable
    medium_deadline = t_start + 0.80 * usable
    variant_deadline = t_start + 0.92 * usable
    final_deadline = min(return_deadline - 5.0, t_start + usable)

    parent = None
    if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
        parent = _normalize_probs(globals().get("height_sequence_1"))

    if parent is None:
        # Safe nontrivial fallback start: optimized smooth medium later.
        parent = np.ones(4096) / 4096.0

    best_a = parent.copy()
    best_sc = _compute_exact_score(best_a)
    print(f"Initial best: {best_sc:.10f}, n={len(best_a)}")

    # Quick symmetrization.
    sym_parent = _normalize_probs(0.5 * (parent + parent[::-1]))
    if sym_parent is not None:
        sc_sym = _compute_exact_score(sym_parent)
        if sc_sym < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_sym
            best_a = sym_parent.copy()
            print(f"Sym parent improves to {best_sc:.10f}")

    extra_candidates = [parent.copy(), best_a.copy()]
    if sym_parent is not None:
        extra_candidates.append(sym_parent.copy())

    eval_cost_full = _estimate_objective_cost(best_a, 1000.0)
    print(f"Estimated eval cost (full): {eval_cost_full:.5f}s/call")

    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main full-size L-BFGS polish from the known parent/best.
    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < main_deadline - 30.0:
        main_betas = [100, 1000, 5000, 20000, 100000, 500000, 2000000]
        sc, ao = _multi_beta_polish(
            best_a,
            main_betas,
            main_deadline,
            eval_cost_full,
            maxfun_cap=80000,
            gtol=1.0e-9,
        )
        if ao is not None and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = ao.copy()
            extra_candidates.append(best_a.copy())
        print(f"After main full polish: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    # Optional medium-size global search if we still need improvement.
    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_sc > 1.5020 and time.monotonic() < medium_deadline - 30.0:
        med_sc, med_a = _medium_search(medium_deadline, rng)
        if med_a is not None and med_sc < np.inf:
            med_full = _interpolate_sequence(med_a, len(best_a))
            if med_full is not None:
                sc_med_full = _compute_exact_score(med_full)
                if sc_med_full < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc_med_full
                    best_a = med_full.copy()
                    extra_candidates.append(best_a.copy())
                    print(f"Medium basis improved after interpolation: {best_sc:.10f}")
                elif sc_med_full < best_sc + 0.05:
                    # Short full-size polish of the medium basis.
                    t_end = min(variant_deadline, time.monotonic() + 300.0)
                    if t_end - time.monotonic() > 30.0:
                        sc2, ao2 = _multi_beta_polish(
                            med_full,
                            [5000, 50000, 500000],
                            t_end,
                            eval_cost_full,
                            maxfun_cap=30000,
                            gtol=1.0e-9,
                        )
                        if ao2 is not None:
                            sc2 = min(sc2, _compute_exact_score(ao2))
                            if sc2 < best_sc:
                                best_sc = sc2
                                best_a = ao2.copy()
                                extra_candidates.append(best_a.copy())
                                print(f"Medium basis polished: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    # Variants from the current best and parent.
    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < variant_deadline - 30.0:
        variants = _generate_variants(best_a, parent, rng, max_count=14)
        for cand in variants:
            if time.monotonic() > variant_deadline - 30.0:
                break
            sc_c = _compute_exact_score(cand)
            if sc_c < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_c
                best_a = cand.copy()
                extra_candidates.append(best_a.copy())
                print(f"Variant improves: {best_sc:.10f}")

            if sc_c < best_sc + 0.03 and sc_c < 1.55:
                t_end = min(variant_deadline, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
                if t_end - time.monotonic() > 20.0:
                    sc2, ao2, _ = _run_lbfgs_beta(
                        cand, 50000,
                        t_end - time.monotonic(),
                        eval_cost_full,
                        maxfun_cap=20000,
                        gtol=1.0e-9,
                    )
                    if ao2 is not None:
                        sc2 = min(sc2, _compute_exact_score(ao2))
                        if sc2 < best_sc:
                            best_sc = sc2
                            best_a = ao2.copy()
                            extra_candidates.append(best_a.copy())
                            print(f"Variant L-BFGS improves: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final high-beta polish.
    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 90.0:
        final_betas = [200000, 1000000, 5000000]
        sc, ao = _multi_beta_polish(
            best_a,
            final_betas,
            final_deadline - 60.0,
            eval_cost_full,
            maxfun_cap=120000,
            gtol=1.0e-10,
        )
        if ao is not None and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = ao.copy()
            extra_candidates.append(best_a.copy())
        print(f"After final high beta polish: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final exact active-peak multiplicative polishing.
    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 20.0:
        for top_k in (3, 5, 10, 20):
            if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 20.0:
                break
            remaining = final_deadline - 15.0 - time.monotonic()
            if remaining < 10.0:
                break
            chunk = min(180.0, max(15.0, remaining / 4.0))
            t_end = min(final_deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + chunk)
            if t_end - time.monotonic() < 5.0:
                break
            sc_p, a_p = _peak_multiplicative_polish(
                best_a, top_k,
                deadline=t_end,
                n_iters=10000,
                eta=0.18,
            )
            if a_p is not None and sc_p < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_p
                best_a = a_p.copy()
                extra_candidates.append(best_a.copy())
                print(f"Peak polish top_k={top_k}: {best_sc:.10f}")

    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    # Verify a few candidates with the exact evaluator and choose best.
    # --------------------------------------------------------------
    best_out = None
    best_verified = np.inf

    pool = [best_a]
    if parent is not None:
        pool.append(parent)
    if best_a is not None:
        pool.append(_normalize_probs(0.5 * (best_a + best_a[::-1])))
    for x in extra_candidates:
        pool.append(x)
        if len(pool) >= 8:
            break

    # First pass: verify up to four distinct-ish candidates.
    verified_count = 0
    for cand in pool:
        if verified_count >= 4:
            break
        cand = _normalize_probs(cand)
        if cand is None:
            continue
        out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in cand]
        if len(out) == 0 or sum(out) < 0.01:
            continue
        v = _safe_evaluate(out)
        verified_count += 1
        print(f"Verified candidate score={v:.10f}, n={len(out)}")
        if v < best_verified:
            best_verified = v
            best_out = out

    # If not yet below target and time remains, do one more verification-guided polish.
    attempt = 0
    while (
        best_verified > 1.5027 - 1.0e-7
        and time.monotonic() < return_deadline - 80
        and attempt < 2
    ):
        attempt += 1
        base = _normalize_probs(best_out) if best_out is not None else best_a
        if base is None:
            base = best_a
        if base is None:
            base = np.ones(1024) / 1024.0

        t_end = min(return_deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + 240.0)
        if t_end - time.monotonic() > 30.0:
            sc, ao = _multi_beta_polish(
                base,
                [200000, 1000000],
                t_end,
                eval_cost_full,
                maxfun_cap=60000,
                gtol=1.0e-10,
            )
            if ao is not None:
                sc = min(sc, _compute_exact_score(ao))
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = ao.copy()
                    extra_candidates.append(best_a.copy())

            te = min(return_deadline - 45.0, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
            if te - time.monotonic() > 10.0:
                sc_p, a_p = _peak_multiplicative_polish(
                    best_a, 5,
                    deadline=te,
                    n_iters=3000,
                    eta=0.12,
                )
                if a_p is not None and sc_p < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc_p
                    best_a = a_p.copy()
                    extra_candidates.append(best_a.copy())

        # Re-verify best_a and parent.
        for cand in (best_a, parent, _normalize_probs(0.5 * (best_a + best_a[::-1]))):
            cand = _normalize_probs(cand)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in cand]
            if len(out) == 0 or sum(out) < 0.01:
                continue
            v = _safe_evaluate(out)
            print(f"Re-checked candidate score={v:.10f}")
            if v < best_verified:
                best_verified = v
                best_out = out

    # Final fallback.
    if best_out is None:
        fb = best_a
        if fb is None:
            fb = np.ones(1024) / 1024.0
        fb = _normalize_probs(fb)
        if fb is None:
            fb = np.ones(64) / 64.0
        best_out = [float(max(0.0, min(1.0, x))) for x in fb]

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    final_val = _safe_evaluate(best_out)
    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"FINAL internal best={best_sc:.10f}, final verified={final_val:.10f}")
    print(f"Length={len(best_out)}, elapsed={elapsed:.1f}s / {total_budget:.0f}s")
    print("=" * 60)

    return best_out
```
